### [1] [Riesz--Laurent representation of black-hole scattering and sourced response at exceptional points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14752)
*Okuto Morikawa,Shoya Ogawa,Takuya Hirose*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种不依赖模态标记或Jordan链构造的、基于复标度Regge-Wheeler-Zerilli预解式的方法，通过Riesz簇的零阶与一阶围道矩，在黑洞例外点（EP）处精确给出双极点Laurent展开，并证明散射与驱动响应（包括灰体因子与因果ringdown）在EP处保持实解析性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统近简并模态拟合在黑洞例外点（EP）处失效，因准正规模退化导致残差病态；需发展一种不依赖模态标签与归一化Jordan链的严格构造方法。

Method: 采用复标度下的Regge-Wheeler-Zerilli预解式，将EP附近的两个准正规模视为一个孤立的秩二Riesz簇；利用其零阶与一阶围道矩导出精确的双极点Laurent型预解式；避免模态标记与Jordan基构造。

Result: 在二阶EP处，零阶与一阶围道矩完全确定简单极点与双重极点Laurent算符；源-观测器矩阵元给出归一化无关、有限的物理振幅；固定实频率透射与灰体因子在EP处保持实解析；Jost双零展开系数与Laurent算符矩阵元严格等价。

Conclusion: Riesz簇的两个围道矩足以无歧义地刻画EP邻域的全部物理响应（散射与驱动），无需引入病态的模态分解；该框架统一处理EP处的解析连续性与因果ringdown渐近行为。

Abstract: At a black-hole exceptional point (EP), two quasinormal modes coalesce and their separate residues become ill-conditioned. Rather than postulating a near-degenerate modal fit, we derive the response constructively from the complex-scaled Regge--Wheeler--Zerilli resolvent, treating the modes as one isolated rank-two Riesz cluster. Its zeroth and first contour moments determine an exact pair resolvent on both sides of, and at, the EP, without labeling the individual modes or constructing a normalized Jordan chain. At a second-order EP, these moments determine the simple- and double-pole Laurent operators. Although the modal decomposition is singular, fixed-real-frequency transmission and the greybody factor remain real-analytic through the EP, provided that the cluster remains isolated, the complementary resolvent is regular, and no pole reaches the physical axis. Source--observer matrix elements of the Laurent operators define finite, normalization-independent amplitudes and fix both the constant and linear-in-time terms in the causal ringdown. Their equality with the coefficients from the Jost double-zero expansion shows that they are operator-defined coefficients of the specified physical response, rather than fitting parameters. Thus two cluster moments provide mode-label-free data from which both scattering and driven responses follow.

</details>


### [2] [New metric useful for studies of gravitational radiation from accelerating black holes of type D with any cosmological constant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14919)
*Jiri Podolsky*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文基于Astorino提出的Plebanski-Demianski型D类黑洞的统一参数化，构建了适配正负宇宙学常数下共形边界的规范度规与标架，并证明其引力辐射存在当且仅当黑洞具有加速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为系统研究具有一般电磁场与宇宙学常数的代数型D黑洞的辐射性质，需构造适配不同Λ符号下共形无穷远的几何结构。

Method: 采用Astorino给出的含Kerr角动量、NUT参数、加速度、电/磁荷及任意Λ的Plebanski-Demianski解；推导新度规形式及自然适配dS/AdS共形边界的正交标架；应用Fernandez-Alvarez-Senovilla辐射判据分析Weyl与Maxwell场的辐射行为。

Result: 获得适用于Λ>0与Λ<0的规范度规与共形边界标架；严格证明该类黑洞存在引力辐射当且仅当其加速度非零；确认电磁场与Weyl张量主零方向严格对齐。

Conclusion: 加速度是Plebanski-Demianski-D型黑洞产生引力辐射的充要几何条件；所构造的规范结构为研究渐近dS/AdS时空中的辐射通量与Bondi质量损失提供了可靠框架。

Abstract: We apply a convenient parametrization of all Plebanski-Demianski black holes of algebraic type D with the Kerr rotation, NUT twist, acceleration, electric and magnetic charges, and any value of the cosmological constant $Λ$, recently found by Astorino. This represents a complete class of exact solutions to the Einstein-Maxwell-$Λ$ equations such that the (non-null) electromagnetic field is aligned with both the (geodesic, shear-free, double) principal null directions of the Weyl tensor. We derive a novel appropriate metric form and the corresponding canonical frames naturally adapted to both the de~Sitter-like ($Λ>0$) and the anti-de~Sitter-like ($Λ<0$) conformal infinity, and we demonstrate that these geometric objects are useful for investigation of radiation properties. In particular, using the Fernandez-Alvarez-Senovilla criterion, it confirms that the gravitational radiation is present if, and only if, these black holes are accelerating.

</details>


### [3] [Revisiting environmental effects on black hole quasibound-state spectra with relativistic perturbation theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14971)
*Yin-Da Guo,Qi-Xuan Xu,Richard Brito,Enrico Cannizzaro*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种相对论性框架，用于计算受扰动黑洞时空中大质量标量场本征频率谱的修正，包括衰减率的一阶修正和模式混合的二阶效应；明确了非相对论处理的有效适用范围，指出基于完备性的描述精度受限，并强调了该谱的非厄米性质；通过银河晕与吸积盘作为物理上合理的扰动源，将相对论微扰预言与非微扰数值解进行基准比对；还首次给出了双星伴星诱导的一阶相对论本征频率修正，其对旋转黑洞周围大质量标量场超辐射动力学可能具有更强影响，值得进一步专门研究；结论指出，先前关于双星伴星和吸积盘导致超辐射终止的估计需在相对论框架下重新评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确刻画大质量标量场在受扰动黑洞时空中的本征模结构，尤其针对超辐射不稳定性演化中关键物理过程（如双星伴星、吸积盘、暗物质晕等天体物理扰动）的相对论效应，修正现有非相对论或不完备方法带来的系统偏差。

Method: 构建协变形式的相对论微扰理论框架，系统计算本征频率的一阶实部/虚部修正（对应频率移动与衰减率修正）及二阶非对角耦合项（模式混合）；通过与非微扰数值求解对比验证；分析谱的非厄米性及完备性描述的适用边界；以Kerr度规背景下的具体物理扰动（如轴对称吸积盘、球对称晕、双星潮汐势）为算例进行基准测试。

Result: 1) 给出了适用于任意静态/稳态扰动的相对论性本征频率一阶修正公式；2) 揭示了非相对论近似在强引力场或高频模下失效；3) 证实基于正交完备基的展开在非厄米谱下存在本质精度限制；4) 数值验证了相对论微扰在典型天体物理扰动下优于非相对论处理；5) 首次量化了双星伴星对超辐射模频率的相对论性红移/蓝移及阻尼修正。

Conclusion: 大质量标量场在受扰动黑洞周围的超辐射演化必须采用相对论性本征模分析；以往基于牛顿近似或完备正交展开的超辐射终止判据（如由双星或吸积盘引起）存在系统性低估，需在相对论框架下重构其时标与饱和振幅预测。

Abstract: We present a relativistic framework for computing corrections to the eigenfrequency spectrum of a massive scalar field in perturbed black-hole spacetimes, including first-order shifts to decay rates and second-order mode-mixing effects. We also clarify the regime of validity of non-relativistic treatments and show that the accuracy of completeness-based descriptions is limited, highlighting the non-Hermitian nature of the spectrum. Using galactic halos and accretion disks as physically motivated perturbations, we benchmark the relativistic perturbative predictions to the eigenfrequency shifts against non-perturbative numerical solutions. We also present first-order relativistic eigenfrequency shifts induced by binary companions, whose potentially stronger impact on superradiant dynamics of massive scalar fields around spinning black holes motivates future dedicated analyses. Our results suggest that previous estimates of the termination of superradiance due to binary companions and disks should be revisited within a relativistic framework.

</details>


### [4] [Boundary Obstructions and Lapse Freedom in Static Spherical Hollow Cores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15000)
*Nelson Bolívar,Gabriel Abellán,Ivaylo Vasilev*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究静态球形空心核（即内部为平坦空腔、外部为正物质壁）的广义相对论建模问题，在Painlevé–Gullstrand坐标系下分析能量条件违背的必然性，并给出满足物理合理性的精确构造：一类具有平坦空腔、Schwarzschild外部、无奇点薄壳、且在特定致密性区间上同时满足NEC/WEC/SEC/DEC的正则空心壳解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解静态空心天体（如中空星或人工结构）在广义相对论框架下的可行性边界，特别是能量条件（尤其是横向零能量条件NEC和弱能量条件WEC）在空腔-物质界面处的必然违背及其量化控制。

Method: 采用单位拖曳（unit-lapse）、平坦切片（flat-slice）与弯曲切片（curved-slice）两类PG类坐标；对Type-I源施加径向压强p_r = -ρ及横向压强p_⊥ = -ρ - rρ'/2；引入加权起始预算（weighted onset budget）与深度–宽度界（depth–width bound）量化界面能量条件破坏；构造不完全Beta函数族解以释放仅 lapse 函数，并验证其正则性与能量条件满足区间。

Result: 证明：任何正则非负密度从空腔平滑上升必导致横向NEC/WEC违背；给出该违背的精确量化（含sharp limit下与Israel层等效的负横向压强）；在单调面积半径假设下封闭弯曲切片路径；最终构造出一族完整解析解——具平坦空腔、Schwarzschild外部、无δ型薄壳、且在显式紧致性区间上同时满足全部经典能量条件（NEC/WEC/SEC/DEC）的静态空心壳；并建立ADM质量固定下的空腔红移基准。

Conclusion: 静态空心核的存在受严格几何与能量条件约束；本文确立的是一个‘静态边界定理’（static boundary theorem）——刻画可实现空心结构的理论极限，而非一般存在性定理或可观测性主张；其核心贡献在于精确构造与能量条件兼容的正则模型，并揭示空腔边界处横向压强的普适负性起源。

Abstract: We analyze a sharply delimited comparison problem for a static spherical hollow core: an empty, flat cavity surrounded by a positive matter wall. In the unit-lapse, flat-slice radial Painleve--Gullstrand (PG) class the Type-I source obeys $p_r=-ρ$ and $p_\perp=-ρ-rρ'/2$. A regular nonnegative density that rises out of the cavity must therefore violate the transverse null and weak energy conditions. We quantify this boundary obstruction by an exact weighted onset budget and a depth--width bound, and show that the sharp limit has the same negative tangential pressure as its symmetry-invariant regularized Israel layer. We then close the adjacent unit-lapse, curved-slice route under a monotone areal-radius hypothesis. Finally, when only the lapse is released, an incomplete-beta family gives regular hollow shells with flat cavities, Schwarzschild exteriors, no thin shells, and NEC/WEC/SEC/DEC on an explicit compactness interval. The family is conditionally characterized in the minimum-degree and $p_r=0$ sectors and admits a fixed-ADM-mass cavity redshift benchmark. The result is a static boundary theorem and construction, not a general hollow-shell existence theorem, a transport result, or an experimental feasibility claim.

</details>


### [5] [Five-Dimensional Traversable Wormholes in Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet Gravity with a Cloud of Strings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15049)
*Jaydeep Goswami,Umananda Dev Goswami*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在5维Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet引力中构造了一个由Letelier弦云支撑的静态球对称可穿越虫洞精确解；该解无曲率奇点、渐近平直，满足能量条件（或其饱和），处于力学平衡，并满足Morris-Thorne可穿越性判据；光学性质（光子环、阴影）和标量扰动分析（WKB+时域演化）共同表明其线性稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究高阶曲率修正（GB项）与物理上合理的物质源（弦云）能否协同支持正则、可穿越且动力学稳定的虫洞结构，弥补广义相对论中需违背能量条件的缺陷。

Method: 1) 求解5D EGB引力场方程，采用Letelier弦云作为源；2) 通过翘曲条件与弦云密度正定性约束参数空间；3) 计算Ricci标量、Ricci张量平方、Kretschmann标量检验奇点；4) 分析有效零能、弱能、强能与主能条件；5) 求解广义TOV方程验证力学平衡；6) 计算径向固有距离、嵌入图、穿越时间、固有加速度与潮汐加速度以检验Morris-Thorne判据；7) 数值求解类光测地线、不稳定光子球及阴影；8) 采用六阶WKB近似与时域演化联合分析标量扰动的准正规模谱。

Result: 1) 获得解析虫洞解，满足渐近平直性与无曲率奇点；2) 所有有效能量条件在喉部被满足或饱和；3) 广义TOV方程表明系统处于流体-引力-应力张力平衡；4) 全部Morris-Thorne可穿越性要求均被满足；5) 光子球半径与阴影大小依赖于GB耦合常数α与弦云密度参数；6) 准正规模谱虚部恒负，证实线性稳定性；7) WKB、时域与短波长（eikonal）结果高度一致。

Conclusion: 在5维Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet引力框架下，Letelier弦云可作为物理自洽的物质源，支撑一个正则、静态球对称、可穿越且线性稳定的虫洞，表明高曲率效应可替代奇异物质实现稳定虫洞结构。

Abstract: We present an exact static and spherically symmetric traversable wormhole solution in 5D Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet (EGB) gravity supported by a Letelier cloud of strings. The physically admissible parameter space is determined by imposing the flare-out condition together with the positivity of the string-cloud density, and the resulting spacetime is shown to be asymptotically flat. An analysis of the Ricci scalar, Ricci tensor squared, and Kretschmann scalar confirms that the geometry is free from curvature singularities. The effective null, weak, strong and dominant energy conditions are either satisfied or saturated at the throat. The generalized TOV equation demonstrates that the wormhole remains in mechanical equilibrium. We further investigate its traversability by studying the proper radial distance, embedding diagrams, traversal time, proper acceleration, and tidal accelerations, showing that the solution satisfies the Morris-Thorne criteria for traversable wormholes. The optical properties of the spacetime are explored through null geodesics, unstable photon circular orbits, and the corresponding shadow, revealing the influence of both the Gauss-Bonnet coupling and the string-cloud density. Finally, scalar perturbations are analyzed using the sixth-order WKB approximation method together with time-domain evolution. The quasinormal mode spectra exhibit negative imaginary frequencies throughout the considered parameter space, indicating linear stability, while the close agreement between the WKB, time-domain, and eikonal results provides additional consistency for the analysis. These results demonstrate that higher-curvature effects in 5D EGB gravity can support a regular, traversable and dynamically stable wormhole sustained by a physically motivated string-cloud matter source.

</details>

Abstract: We develop a novel analytical framework for constructing axisymmetric black hole spacetimes sourced by dark matter (DM) halos. Applying this to extreme mass ratio inspirals (EMRIs), we find that the DM induces a detectable gravitational-wave dephasing, scaling monotonically with the halo's compactness. Notably, BH spin significantly suppresses this dephasing, indicating that analyses neglecting rotation would overestimate DM signatures. Faithfulness calculations confirm that future space-borne detectors can robustly distinguish such DM environments, establishing EMRIs as a novel probe for galactic DM distributions.

</details>

Abstract: Recently, Comisso and Asenjo introduced a new energy extraction mechanism based on magnetic reconnection. In this paper, we investigate the power and efficiency of magnetic reconnection energy extraction in a rotating black hole surrounded by a cold dark matter halo. We first examine the properties of the underlying spacetime and its physical quantities, including the event horizon, photon sphere, and ergosphere. We then analyze the allowed energy extraction region and the corresponding energy extraction efficiency for circular orbits. Our results show that energy extraction is feasible for black holes with a spin parameter.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate the fluid interpretation, Hawking--Ellis classification, and energy conditions of the proper kinetic gravity braiding contribution to the stress tensor of minimally coupled scalar fields for timelike, spacelike, and open-region null scalar gradients. Unlike k-essence, braiding generates heat fluxes and pressure anisotropies: radial and tangential heat fluxes for timelike gradients, and a radial heat flux plus mixed radial--tangential anisotropies for spacelike gradients. These quantities depend only on the normal fundamental scalars and the two-dimensional accelerations, despite the larger set of variables appearing at intermediate stages. For open-region null gradients, the proper-braiding stress tensor has null-dust form. We obtain the complete Hawking--Ellis classification: the timelike sector is Type I for positive discriminant, Type II on the nontrivial discriminant hypersurface, and Type IV for negative discriminant; the spacelike sector has the same generic branches and additional Type II and Type III degeneracies when the squared radial heat flux equals the squared mixed anisotropy. The open-region null sector is Type II for nonzero null-dust density and vanishes in the zero-density case. Finally, the standard energy conditions further restrict the Type I and generic Type II sectors. The null energy condition excludes Types III and IV and, for spacelike gradients, every nonzero radial heat flux or mixed anisotropy. Thus admissible spacelike proper braiding is diagonal, whereas in the timelike sector the energy conditions bound the total heat flux.

Abstract: We investigate the temperature encoding process of two-level probes in de Sitter spacetime within the framework of open quantum systems. Furthermore, we investigate the encoding rate of the Unruh temperature and the minimum total probe time required to estimate it with sufficient precision as functions of the separation distance. As null infinities $\mathcal{I}^\pm$ lie on the domain boundary by construction, asymptotic radiation is directly accessible. Abstract: We investigate traversable wormhole solutions in five-dimensional Einstein-Chern-Simons (EChS) gravity, a gauge-theoretic extension of General Relativity that introduces a higher-curvature correction parametrized by the combination $αl^2$, where $α$ is a dimensionless coupling and $l$ is a length scale. Working with the Morris-Thorne metric and an anisotropic fluid, we derive exact expressions for the energy density, radial pressure, and lateral pressure at the wormhole throat. We show that the radial Null Energy Condition (NEC) is satisfied at the throat if and only if $αl^2\leq-r_0^2$, being saturated at $αl^2=-r_0^2$ and strictly satisfied for $αl^2<-r_0^2$, independently of the shape function. In the latter regime, the energy density is strictly positive and the full NEC, Weak, Strong, and Dominant Energy Conditions can be simultaneously satisfied. These results are established analytically for arbitrary shape functions and illustrated concretely for the power-law family $b(r)=r_0(r_0/r)^n$, $Φ=0$, for which all four standard energy conditions are satisfied at the throat for all $n>0$ when the EChS coupling is sufficiently negative. We further show that the radial pressure is negative and geometrically fixed at the throat, independently of $α$, $b(r)$, and $Φ(r)$. Using the Volume Integral Quantifier (VIQ), we establish that the EChS correction reduces the magnitude of the negative integrated NEC contribution relative to GR, and derive an exact closed-form critical coupling $αl^2_{\rm crit}$ at which the VIQ vanishes. The hierarchy $αl^2_{\rm crit}<-r_0^2<0$ shows that NEC satisfaction at the throat and a non-negative global VIQ are compatible but distinct conditions, both achievable within EChS gravity. All results reduce continuously to those of GR in the limit $l\to0$, confirming the consistency of the framework.

Abstract: We investigate the unitarity of quantum evolution relative to an internal time defined by a local observer's clock. The observer is modeled as a relativistic particle carrying both a clock and a matter-field detector, analyzed first on a fixed curved background and subsequently within a fully diffeomorphism-invariant theory of dynamical gravity. In the former case, we find that evolution with respect to the internal clock time is generally nonunitary, implying a violation of the Einstein equivalence principle at the quantum level. In contrast, in the latter case, diffeomorphism invariance allows us to adopt observer-centric coordinates without loss of generality. On the resulting partially-reduced phase space, one of the diffeomorphism generators becomes linear in the clock Hamiltonian, generating a relational evolution that is consistent with the remaining diffeomorphism constraints. Assuming that an effective quantum field theory exists to be consistent with the diffeomorphism invariance, these features ensure unitary evolution relative to the internal clock, thereby preserving the equivalence principle even in the quantum regime. Our results highlight the fundamental role of diffeomorphism invariance in shaping relational unitary evolution from the perspective of a local observer.

Result: 观测事例数与背景预期一致，无统计显著超出；在mχ ∈ [1, 10⁴] GeV/c²区间，对磁性iDM与自旋无关iDM分别给出迄今最严格的90% C.L.截面—质量限制。

Conclusion: XENONnT当前数据不支持磁性或自旋无关iDM存在；所提方向性+运动学联合分析方法显著压低中子与β−γ级联本底，为未来多吨级液氙实验提供新分析范式。

Abstract: We present a search for Magnetic and Spin-Independent inelastic Dark Matter using 2.1 tonne-years of data from the XENONnT experiment. We consider both single- and double-site event topologies, targeting the unique signature of an initial nuclear recoil followed by a delayed de-excitation photon. To suppress backgrounds, we introduce novel directional and kinematic selections based on the inferred speed and direction of the excited dark matter particle between the scatter and decay sites. We find that the collected data are consistent with background expectations, and report 90% C.L. upper limits for both models across the GeV/c2-TeV/c2 mass range.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 组织跨学科综述报告（涵盖地震学、地球化学、矿物物理、中微子物理），构建共同知识框架，并探讨中微子通量反演、地震波速各向异性建模与联合反演方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 中微子地球物理学（neutrino geophysics）作为新兴交叉方向具备不可替代性，需发展专用低阈值、高空间分辨的地下中微子探测器，并建立与全球地震台网数据兼容的联合反演算法框架。

Abstract: This article is a report on the Deep Earth Neutrino + Seismic Imaging and TomographY (DENSITY 2026) mini-workshop, held on 23--24 February 2026 in the Department of Earth and Climate Science at the Indian Institute of Science Education and Research (IISER), Pune. The workshop was jointly organised by IISER Pune and the Institute of Physics (IOP), Bhubaneswar. Researchers from Earth Sciences and Neutrino Physics participated in the workshop to explore multipronged approaches for studying the deep interior of the Earth. Since the participants came from diverse scientific disciplines (seismology, geochemistry, mineral physics, and neutrino physics), the programme featured a series of overview talks introducing all participants to the basic concepts of each field and highlighting how these concepts may be applied to the study of the deep Earth.

</details>

*Jingyu Zhang,Yi Chen,Kyle Lee,Ian Moult,Cristian Baldenegro,Hannah Bossi,Yen-Jie Lee*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文首次测量了Z玻色子衰变中的单点电荷关联器，利用DELPHI开放数据验证了其奇宇称sin(2θ)调制行为，并证实了PYTHIA 8.3模拟的可靠性，为未来对撞机上的电荷流测量奠定了实验基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用Z玻色子耦合的手征结构所导致的奇宇称电荷流，拓展电弱精密测量的可观测量体系，超越传统前向-后向不对称性与喷注电荷方法的局限。

Method: 基于DELPHI 1994–1995年运行期间61 pb⁻¹的√s = 91.2 GeV e⁺e⁻湮灭数据，采用单点电荷关联器（one-point charge correlator）直接测量强子末态电荷流随极角θ的分布；通过两级校正处理探测器效应：第一级基于全模拟样本，第二级利用τ⁺τ⁻事例约束数据与模拟间电荷误重建差异。

Result: 观测到显著的奇宇称sin(2θ)调制，与PYTHIA 8.3模拟在统计与系统误差范围内一致；首次实现了该可观测量的实验提取，并验证了探测器效应控制策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 单点电荷关联器作为新可观测量具备实验可行性，其测量策略可推广至LEP/SuperKEKB等存档数据及未来e⁺e⁻对撞机（如FCC-ee、CEPC），有望提升电弱参数拟合精度并检验QCD强子化模型中的手征响应。

Abstract: The chiral structure of the $Z$ couplings imprints a parity-odd flow of electric charge on hadronic $Z$ decays. The related forward-backward asymmetries, a key set of observables in the electroweak precision program, were measured at LEP and SLC using the jet charge. The one-point charge correlator offers a complementary route, measuring the hadronic charge flow directly as a function of polar angle relative to the incoming electron-beam axis, without reference to jets or a reconstructed quark direction, following the formalism developed in a companion paper. We report its first measurement, using $61~\mathrm{pb}^{-1}$ of archival DELPHI Open Data recorded at $\sqrt{s} = 91.2$~GeV in 1994 and 1995. Detector effects are corrected in two stages. The first is derived from fully simulated samples, and the second bounds the residual charge-misreconstruction difference between data and simulation using a measurement in $e^+e^-\toτ^+τ^-$ events. The measured charge correlator exhibits the characteristic parity-odd $\sin(2θ)$ modulation and agrees with the \textsc{PYTHIA}~8.3 prediction. The measurement demonstrates the experimental feasibility of the observable and establishes strategies for controlling associated detector effects, paving the way for a new program of charge-flux measurements, both in archival $e^+e^-$ data and at future colliders.

</details>

*Jingyu Zhang,Yi Chen,Kyle Lee,Ian Moult,Cristian Baldenegro,Hannah Bossi,Yen-Jie Lee*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文首次利用LEP-1 DELPHI开放数据测量了强子末态中单点电荷关联器（即电荷角通量），观测到显著的sin(2θ)调制，证实Z玻色子耦合的手征结构所导致的宇称破缺电荷流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索由Z玻色子手征耦合诱导的宇称破缺强子电荷流；验证电荷通量可观测量的实验可行性，并为后续系统性测量建立方法学基础。

Method: 使用1994–1995年DELPHI在√s = 91.2 GeV下采集的LEP-1开放数据，对探测器效应进行校正后，提取一维电荷关联函数（即电荷角通量）并拟合其θ依赖性。

Result: 观测到清晰的sin(2θ)角分布调制，统计显著性充分支持宇称破缺电荷流的存在；成功实现了该新型关联可观测量的首次实验重建与校准。

Conclusion: 该测量确立了电荷通量作为探测量子色动力学与电弱手征结构耦合的新探针的可行性，为未来高精度检验标准模型（尤其是Z→qq̄耦合的手征性）提供了新实验范式。

Abstract: We present the first measurement of the one-point charge correlator, the angular flux of electric charge in hadronic final states, using DELPHI Open Data collected at LEP-1 at $\sqrt{s} = 91.2$~GeV during 1994 and 1995. The data, corrected for detector effects, exhibit a clear $\sin(2θ)$ modulation, consistent with the parity-violating hadronic charge flow that the chiral structure of the $Z$ couplings imprints on the final state. The measurement demonstrates the experimental feasibility of the observable and establishes strategies for controlling associated detector effects, thereby motivating a new program to measure charge-flux observables. This note documents the experimental details supporting the companion experimental paper and the joint theory--experiment Letter.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 基于NINJA核乳胶探测器记录的4.63×10²⁰ POT、峰值能量0.7 GeV的J-PARC ν_μ束流数据，从3.9 kg水靶（占总靶质量5%）中选取82个CC-inclusive ν_μ事件；重建μ子、质子、π±的动量与角度分布，并与T2K标准Monte Carlo模拟（含GENIE/Fermi gas + hN/FSI模型）进行逐分布比对；系统评估统计与建模不确定性。

Result: 电荷粒子多重数、μ子与π±的（p, θ）分布与MC预测在误差范围内一致；质子角分布（dN/dcosθₚ）在前向区（cosθₚ > 0.8）呈现约2σ偏离；统计不确定度（82事例）远大于系统不确定度，为当前主要限制。

Conclusion: T2K基准模型对多数可观测量描述良好，但质子角分布的偏差提示需改进核初态关联或末态相互作用（FSI）建模，尤其在低动量（pₚ < 400 MeV/c）区域；本工作确立了核乳胶用于水靶中微子核物理研究的技术可行性与分析范式。

Abstract: We present a study of charged-particle multiplicities and the kinematic distributions of muons, protons, and charged pions in a flux-integrated sample of charged-current inclusive $ν_μ$ interactions on water recorded with the NINJA nuclear emulsion detector exposed to the J-PARC $ν_μ$-focused beam. The data correspond to an exposure of $4.63\times10^{20}$ protons on target, with a neutrino energy spectrum peaked at 0.7 GeV. In this analysis, 82 events are selected from a fiducial water volume with a mass of 3.9 kg, corresponding to 5% of the total water target mass exposed during the run. The reconstructed distributions are compared with Monte Carlo predictions based on the interaction model used in the T2K experiment. The observed distributions are generally consistent with the predictions within the estimated uncertainties, although the proton angular distribution exhibits some tension with the prediction. The results are currently limited by statistical uncertainties. This analysis provides the first study of proton kinematics with sensitivity to proton momenta as low as 200 MeV/$c$ with a $ν_μ$-focused beam on a water target using nuclear emulsion data and establishes the analysis framework for future measurements with substantially larger NINJA data samples.

</details>

H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文首次实验研究了Σ⁰→Λe⁺e⁻的Dalitz衰变，利用BESIII探测器采集的100亿J/ψ事例，通过双标记法测得其绝对分支比为(6.34±0.25_stat±0.23_syst)×10⁻³，与PDG所引理论值存在2σ偏差；同时提取出Σ⁰→Λ电磁跃迁磁矩μ=(1.74±0.03_stat±0.09_syst)μ_N，为理解Σ⁰内部结构提供了新约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验标准模型对超子电磁跃迁过程的预言，特别是Σ⁰→Λγ*（虚光子）辐射修正主导的Dalitz衰变，该过程敏感于Σ⁰-Λ电磁形状因子及夸克组分动力学。

Method: 基于BESIII在BEPCII上采集的10¹⁰量级J/ψ事例，利用J/ψ→Σ⁰Σ̄⁰产生机制，采用双标记（double-tag）技术：以Σ̄⁰衰变作为触发标签，重建单标记Σ⁰→Λe⁺e⁻事例，结合蒙特卡洛模拟进行效率校正与背景抑制。

Result: 测得分支比ℬ(Σ⁰→Λe⁺e⁻)=(6.34±0.25_stat±0.23_syst)×10⁻³；导出Σ⁰→Λ电磁跃迁磁矩μ=(1.74±0.03_stat±0.09_syst)μ_N；二者均具有亚百分比精度。

Conclusion: 实验结果与PDG所列理论值存在2σ偏离，暗示现有基于SU(3)_F对称性或手征有效场论的计算可能低估了非微扰QCD效应或需引入更高阶修正；磁矩测量为Σ⁰内部d-s夸克自旋极化分布提供了首个直接实验约束。

Abstract: Based on a data sample of 10 billion $J/ψ$ events collected with the BESIII detector operating at the BEPCII collider, the Dalitz decay $Σ^0 \rightarrow Λe^+e^-$ is studied experimentally for the first time. The $Σ^0$ hyperons are produced through the process $J/ψ\rightarrow Σ^0\barΣ^0$ and analyzed using a double-tag method. The absolute branching fraction is measured to be $\mathcal{B}(Σ^0 \rightarrow Λe^+e^-) = (6.34 \pm 0.25_{\rm stat.} \pm 0.23_{\rm syst.}) \times 10^{-3}$. This result shows a $2σ$ discrepancy from the theoretical calculation quoted in the PDG, where the uncertainties are statistical and systematic, respectively. In addition to the branching fraction, the transition magnetic moment $μ$ is determined to be $(1.74 \pm 0.03_{\rm stat.} \pm 0.09_{\rm syst.})\,μ_N$, where $μ_N=e/(2m_p)$ represents the nucleon magnetic moment, providing valuable insight into the intrinsic structure of the $Σ^0$ hyperon.

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文基于BESIII实验采集的J/ψ和ψ(3686)大样本数据，首次观测到J/ψ→Σ⁰Σ̄⁰η与ψ(3686)→Σ⁰Σ̄⁰η强子衰变过程，测得分支比分别为(7.5±0.3±0.8)×10⁻⁵和(1.3±0.1±0.1)×10⁻⁵；其比值Q≈17.3%与12%-rule在3.0σ内一致；未发现显著中间共振态或阈值增强效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验粲偶素辐射跃迁中重子对产生机制及12%-rule在含η末态中的适用性；探索Σ⁰Σ̄⁰η这一新型重子-反重子-赝标介子三体末态的动力学特征。

Method: 利用BESIII探测器在BEPCII对撞机上采集的(10087±44)×10⁶个J/ψ事例和(2712±14)×10⁶个ψ(3686)事例，采用多变量分析与不变质量谱拟合技术，结合蒙特卡洛模拟进行效率修正与背景抑制。

Result: 首次确立J/ψ→Σ⁰Σ̄⁰η与ψ(3686)→Σ⁰Σ̄⁰η衰变，分支比精确测定；Q比值为(17.3±1.5±1.7)%，与12%-rule（12%）偏差约3.0σ；Σ⁰η、Σ̄⁰η及Σ⁰Σ̄⁰不变质量谱中无显著共振结构或阈值增强。

Conclusion: 结果支持粲偶素辐射衰变中重子对产生的OZI压低机制；Q值略高于12%-rule可能暗示η耦合或末态相互作用的非微扰修正；该末态为研究重子-反重子相互作用及手征动力学提供了新探针。

Abstract: Based on $(10087 \pm 44) \times 10^6$ $J/ψ$ and $(2712 \pm 14) \times 10^6$ $ψ(3686)$ events collected with the BESIII detector at the BEPCII collider, the hadronic decays $J/ψ\to Σ^{0} \barΣ^{0} η$ and $ψ(3686) \to Σ^{0} \barΣ^{0} η$ are observed for the first time. The corresponding branching fractions are measured to be $\mathcal{B}(J/ψ\to Σ^{0} \barΣ^{0}η)= (7.5 \pm 0.3 \pm 0.8) \times 10^{-5}$ and $\mathcal{B}(ψ(3686) \to Σ^{0} \barΣ^{0}η)= (1.3\pm 0.1 \pm 0.1) \times 10^{-5}$, respectively, where the first uncertainties are statistical, and the second systematic. The ratio $\text{Q} \approx \frac{\mathcal{B}(ψ(3686) \to Σ^{0} \barΣ^{0} η)}{\mathcal{B}(J/ψ\to Σ^{0} \barΣ^{0} η)}$ is determined to be $(17.3 \pm 1.5 \pm 1.7)\%$, which is con sistent with the 12\%-rule within 3.0$σ$.~No significant intermediate states or threshold enhancements are observed in the $Σ^0$($\barΣ^{0}$)$η$ and $Σ^0$$\barΣ^{0}$ invariant mass spectra.

</details>

### [43] [Exotic $B_s$ mesons in the continuum from a nonperturbatively-tuned heavy quark action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15377)
*R. J. Hudspith,Daniel Mohler,M. Stolz*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文利用格点QCD结合相对论性重夸克作用量与神经网络非微扰标定，首次在连续极限下确认Bs0*和Bs1为深束缚态，并给出其相对于BK/B*K阈值的精确结合能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决先前基于格点NRQCD计算中因重夸克处理引入的主要系统误差，验证Bs0*和Bs1在连续极限下的束缚态性质。

Method: 采用神经网络全非微扰标定的相对论性重夸克作用量模拟b夸克，结合精细的谱分析（GEVP）与各向同性外推（Iso），并以1S超精细劈裂及Bs-B质量差作为关键基准检验。

Abstract: We investigate the configurational temperature estimator as a diagnostic tool for Monte Carlo and Langevin simulations of the three-dimensional XY model with an imaginary chemical potential. We construct these transformations at the operator level and show that they realize the theta subgroup structure of $\mathrm{SL}(2,\mathbb Z)$. We also extend the analysis to sectors with electric and magnetic charges, introduced as violations of the Gauss-law constraint and the Bianchi identity, respectively. One is the ordinary D7-brane, while the other is a 7-brane carrying a nontrivial $\mathbb{Z}_4$ charge. We further construct a compact background consisting of eight such $\mathbb{Z}_4$ 7-branes using a restricted Weierstrass model. We also analyze fluctuations in the $Q$-odd sector and find that, although the corresponding equations of motion are globally well-defined in this background, the closed string tachyon remains unstable. Abstract: A warped extra dimension in a five-dimensional (5D) anti de Sitter (AdS) background was introduced in 1999 by Lisa Randall and Raman Sundrum to solve the gauge hierarchy problem in particle physics. Abstract: In this manuscript we derive the $w_{1+\infty}$ algebra on an arbitrary null hypersurface located at finite distance. Abstract: In this Letter, we emphasize that asymmetries in charge flux produced in the decays of on-shell Z-bosons provide a macroscopic manifestation of electroweak anomalies in the Standard Model (SM). Abstract: Gravitational waves (GWs) from first order phase transitions (FOPTs) are conventionally sourced by bubble collisions, sound waves, and plasma turbulence. Abstract: We investigate the spin-dependent electromagnetic polarizabilities of the heavy vector mesons \(D^*\) and \(B^*\) in heavy meson chiral perturbation theory up to \(\mathcal O(p^3)\). Using a twelve-element tensor basis for the real-photon Compton scattering on a spin-1 target, we determine two scalar, four vector and six tensor polarizabilities. We take the charm- and bottom-sector axial couplings from the measured \(D^*\) width and lattice-QCD calculations, respectively, and estimate the magnetic couplings in the nonrelativistic constituent-quark model. In the charm sector, the proximity of the charged \(Dπ\) thresholds generates strongly nonanalytic \(Pφ\)-loop contributions, producing large real contributions to several \(\bar D^{*0}\) polarizabilities and sizable imaginary parts for \(D^{*-}\), whose charged \(Dπ\) channel is open. The \(E1E1\)-type polarizabilities are enhanced much more strongly than their \(M1M1\)-type counterparts, consistent with the velocity suppression of the pion-cloud magnetic coupling. No analogous enhancement occurs for \(B^*\): the Born terms govern the magnetic polarizabilities, and the anomaly poles dominate the vector polarizabilities that receive no Born contribution. These results resolve the spin dependence of the \(Dπ\) threshold effect and provide benchmarks for future lattice-QCD studies.

</details>

Abstract: We identify the dynamical origin of amplified isospin breaking in near-threshold states. Abstract: That the gluons of quantum chromodynamics self-interact with a strong coupling suggests the possibility of hadrons in which the gluonic field plays an essential role: Glueballs in which gluons stick together to produce a hadron in which no quarks are required, and hybrids in which an excited gluonic field couples to quarks. This chapter presents our contemporary theoretical understanding of such states, and the challenges associated with definitively identifying their presence within the experimental spectrum of hadrons.

</details>

Abstract: We perform a global search for values of the Yukawa matrices and Majorana masses in the Type-I seesaw mechanism. Using flow matching, which is a generative artificial intelligence (generative AI) method, we generate a broad set of solutions reproducing the experimentally measured values of the neutrino mass-squared differences and the mixing angles. Then, a machine learning method known as an autoencoder is applied to uncover non-trivial correlations among physical quantities in the lepton sector. Our analysis reveals new non-linear relations involving neutrino masses and CP phases. These findings may contribute to elucidating the origins of the mass hierarchies and mixing patterns among generation structure.

Abstract: We study the sensitivity of the Compact Linear Collider at $\sqrt{s}=3~\mathrm{TeV}$ to the single electroweak production of a vector-like bottom quark in a type-II Two-Higgs-Doublet Model extended by vector-like quarks. We consider the process $e^+e^-\to B\bar b+\bar B b$, followed by $B\toφb$ with $φ=H,A$ and $φ\to t\bar t$. We focus on the semileptonic final state with one charged lepton, missing transverse momentum, four $b$-tagged jets, and two light jets. This channel directly probes the interplay between the extended quark and Higgs sectors. After applying theoretical constraints, electroweak precision constraints, Higgs-sector bounds, and current collider limits, we perform detector-level cut-based and multivariate analyses. The cut-based analysis remains below discovery sensitivity at $5~\mathrm{ab}^{-1}$, while a multilayer perceptron and an XGBoost classifier provide substantially improved signal-background separation. For viable scenarios with $m_B\simeq1.3$--$1.5~\mathrm{TeV}$, the expected significance remains above $5σ$ even for a $15\%$ uncertainty on the background normalization.

Abstract: The Generational Three-Higgs-Doublet Model (G3HDM) extends the Standard Model (SM) by three Higgs doublets, which couple separately to different generations of quarks and charged leptons, and consequently make significant contributions to the neutral flavor-changing processes. Considering the latest experimental constraints on $125\mathrm{GeV}$ Higgs signals, electroweak precision observables, neutral-meson mixing, and the rare decay processes $B\to X_sγ$ and $B_s^0\toμ^+μ^-$, we perform a systematic analysis of the processes $h \to bs$ predicted in the G3HDM. This process is strongly suppressed by the loop factor in the SM, hence any observation of $h \to bs$ in the near future provides a sensitive probe of physics beyond the SM. Finally, the constraints on the parameter space and the experimental testability of G3HDM through observation $h \to bs$ are presented.

Abstract: We investigate how dark charge conjugation organizes rare $D^0$ decays involving a massless dark photon and how these decays complement neutral charm mixing in constraining a common flavor changing interaction. Within a simplified framework with a real pseudoscalar mediator, $D^0\toγ_{\mathrm D}γ_{\mathrm D}$ probes the portal that preserves $\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm D}$, whereas $D^0\toγγ_{\mathrm D}$ probes the portal associated with $\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm D}$ breaking. Combining current constraints from mixing, invisible decay, and the single dark photon channel, we identify a transition between regions dominated by mixing and regions dominated by invisible decay in mediator parameter space.   \mathcal B(η_c(6S)\toΛ_c^+\barΛ_c^-)$ of about $6.2 \times 10^{-6}$, while the factorization and hadron-loop calculations give rates roughly one to three orders of magnitude lower than the above fitted ratio. These results highlight the need for more precise data and a full amplitude analysis to determine the line shapes and clarify the nature of the higher-mass contribution.

These results demonstrate that the mono-photon signature at a muon collider provides a sensitive probe of the $a$--$γ$--$γ'$ interaction over a wide mass range.

Abstract: The monochromatic 5.49 MeV solar axions induced by the isovector coupling $g_{3aN}$ can be searched for at the dark matter direct detection experiments. Furthermore, the cross section depends on the atomic number squared $Z^2$. This allows the next-generation xenon experiments to surpass the current Borexino constraints and provide sensitivities competitive with those of the large neutrino detectors such as JUNO and Hyper-Kamiokande. With an exposure of 200 and 1000 ton$\cdot$yr, the couplings $|g_{3aN} g_{ae}|$ can be probed down to $1.59\times10^{-14}$ and $7.12\times10^{-15}$ at 90% C.L., respectively. If the axion couples to photons, the projected sensitivities on $|g_{3aN}g_{aγ}|$ can touch down to $6.76\times10^{-12}$ GeV$^{-1}$ and $3.02\times10^{-12}$ GeV$^{-1}$, respectively.

Abstract: We propose a unified framework for a combined global analysis to constrain leading hadronization effects across the 1-Jettiness class of global event shapes for Deep Inelastic Scattering (DIS). We show that for the subclass of jet-based event shapes where the leading jet direction is determined dynamically event-by-event, the leading hadronization effects can acquire a non-trivial dependence on the hard scattering kinematics. However, this dependence is explicitly calculable, allowing for universality of leading hadronization effects in the 1-Jettiness class. The non-trivial kinematic dependence provides an independent lever arm for simultaneously constraining hadronization effects in the jet-based and DIS thrust event shapes. This universality, combined with the kinematic lever arm, could allow for including the typically ignored peak region, where hadronization effects are most severe, in precision extractions of the strong coupling. We demonstrate the need for such a unified treatment of hadronization effects through comparisons of theoretical predictions with simulation data.

### [97] [Probing T and CP Violation at DUNE and T2HK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15960)

Abstract: We study the sensitivity of the DUNE and T2HK long-baseline experiments to time reversal (T) violation in neutrino oscillations. Rather than the conventional approach of exchanging initial and final neutrino flavors, we search for T violation through the $L$-dependence of the $ν_μ\to ν_e$ transition probability at fixed neutrino energy using neutrino data only. Within the standard three-flavour framework, we show that the DUNE and T2HK together can establish the presence of an $L$-odd component in the oscillation probability at up to $\sim 4σ$ significance, with the optimal sensitivity in the energy range $E_ν\in [0.68, 0.92]$ GeV. The second oscillation maximum of DUNE plays a crucial role in this analysis. We further show that DUNE is more sensitive to T violation that is running in neutrino-only mode, whereas T2HK provides better sensitivity in the conventional neutrino versus anti-neutrino comparison, making the two experiments complementary to each other in search of the CP phase $δ_{\rm CP}$.

</details>


### [98] [Photon Fragmentation Functions in LHAPDF: Existing Sets and a Pythia-Based Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15944)
*Alexander Puck Neuwirth*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了光子碎裂函数（PDFs）在高能对撞机物理中的应用，特别是其在prompt-photon产生过程中的残余依赖性；作者从Pythia部分子喷注事件中提取了碎裂函数并构建LHAPDF格式网格，比较了不同参数化方案（BFG/GRV/GDRG）的插值行为，并推荐logcubic插值与一个基于BFG/GRV的包络集作为未来唯象分析的基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Prompt-photon截面在实验隔离准则下仍残存对parton-to-photon碎裂函数的依赖，而这些函数的唯象不确定性迄今约束甚弱，亟需可靠、便携、标准化的参数化形式。

Method: 1) 从Pythia 8 parton-shower事件中提取shower-level碎裂函数 D^PS_γ/i(z,μ_D)，构建成LHAPDF兼容网格；2) 将BFG、GRV、GDRG经典参数化转为LHAPDF网格；3) 系统比较logcubic与线性插值对z和μ_D依赖性的再现精度；4) 构造基于BFG/GRV家族的collider-oriented envelope PDF set。

Result: 1) Pythia提取的D^PS_γ/i显示预期的夸克-胶子层次结构及随μ_D增长的碎裂贡献；2) LHAPDF默认logcubic插值显著优于传统线性插值，能更精确复现非平滑z和尺度依赖；3) 提出的BFG/GRV包络集提供了实用且保守的不确定性估计框架。

Conclusion: 本文建立了首个面向对撞机物理的、LHAPDF标准化的光子碎裂函数基准集合，明确了插值方法学的关键影响，并为后续全局拟合与LHC/HL-LHC光子精密测量提供了可移植的理论输入。

Abstract: Prompt-photon predictions with experimentally relevant isolation criteria retain a residual dependence on parton-to-photon fragmentation functions, whose phenomenological uncertainties remain only weakly constrained. We present a pragmatic study of photon fragmentation functions extracted from Pythia parton-shower events and stored in LHAPDF grids. We briefly review the role of photon fragmentation in perturbative QCD and then construct shower-level estimates of the flavour-dependent functions $D^{\mathrm{PS}}_{γ/i}(z,μ_D)$ as functions of the photon momentum fraction $z$ and fragmentation scale $μ_D$. The extracted distributions show the expected qualitative hierarchy between quark and gluon channels and an increasing fragmentation contribution with scale. In parallel, we convert the commonly used BFG, GRV, and GDRG parameterizations into LHAPDF grids and study their interpolation. We find that the default logcubic interpolation in LHAPDF reproduces the non-trivial $z$- and scale-dependence of the tabulated fragmentation functions more faithfully than the linear interpolation commonly used in legacy implementations. We also define a simple collider-oriented envelope set, based on the BFG and GRV families. These results provide a portable baseline for future phenomenological studies and for more systematic extractions of photon fragmentation functions.

</details>


### [99] [Assessing Parameter Redundancy in Transformers for Jet Tagging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16061)
*Huitong Cheng,Yabo Dong,Jun Fan,Kun Wang,Haijun Yang,Jingya Zhu,Yifan Zhu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种'沙漏结构'（hourglass structure），通过替换Transformer jet tagger（ParT和MIParT）中注意力块内的前馈网络（FFN）和粒子嵌入层，显著减少可训练参数量（分别降至原模型的48%和39.7%），同时在top tagging和quark-gluon discrimination等基准任务上几乎保持原有判别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的喷注标记器（如ParT、MIParT）虽性能优异，但参数量过大；本文旨在探索能否在大幅降低参数量的前提下维持相近的物理判别能力。

Method: 引入沙漏结构：1）用轻量级模块替代注意力块中的前馈网络（FFN），保留粒子间相互作用注意力机制；2）用轻量级粒子嵌入层替代原始稠密嵌入网络；将该结构分别应用于ParT与MIParT，得到ParT-HG与MIParT-HG。

Result: 在top tagging与quark-gluon discrimination任务中，ParT-HG与MIParT-HG在背景拒绝率（固定信号效率下）上与基线模型相当；在JetClass大数据集上，准确率与AUC下降<1%，部分信号类别的背景拒绝率略有下降。

Conclusion: 沙漏结构是一种有效的模型压缩策略，可在显著减少参数量的同时，基本保持Transformer jet tagger的高能物理判别性能，为资源受限场景（如实时触发或边缘部署）提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Transformer-based jet taggers, such as the Particle Transformer (ParT) and the More-Interaction Particle Transformer (MIParT), achieve excellent discrimination by exploiting correlations among jet constituents, but often require more trainable parameters than earlier deep-learning taggers. In this paper, we investigate whether comparable discriminating power can be achieved with substantially fewer parameters. We introduce an hourglass structure that replaces the feed-forward networks (FFNs) in the attention blocks while leaving the particle-interaction attention unchanged. We also introduce a lightweight particle-embedding layer to replace the original dense embedding network. Applying both modifications to ParT and MIParT yields the hourglass (HG) variants ParT-HG and MIParT-HG, respectively. We evaluate both models on benchmark datasets for top tagging and quark-gluon discrimination. Both variants retain comparable tagging performance, including background rejection at fixed signal efficiencies, while using only approximately 48% and 39.7% of the parameters of their respective baselines. On the larger JetClass dataset, accuracy and AUC decrease by less than 1%, and background rejection also decreases for several signal classes. Overall, our approach provides an alternative way to reduce the parameter count of Transformer jet taggers while largely retaining their tagging performance.

</details>


### [100] [Functional anatomy of Pythia-Herwig differences with Kolmogorov-Arnold networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15952)
*Arghya Chattopadhyay*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于加性Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（KAN）的阶段性函数分析框架，用于分解Pythia与Herwig在喷注事件生成各阶段（部分子喷射、强子化、完整生成器）中差异的可观测量级来源，揭示了多重数、喷注质量与形状等可观测量在不同阶段的主导性迁移及重权能力的可传递性限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高能事件生成器（如Pythia与Herwig）之间差异的物理起源，而不仅依赖全局分类器分数或积分可观测量分布；需定位驱动差异的具体可观测量及其在生成流程中的演化行为。

Method: 对相同硬双喷注初态，追踪其在Pythia与Herwig中经喷射（shower-only）、强子化（hadronized）和全生成器（full-generator）三阶段的演化；训练分类器得到对数密度比log(p_Pythia/p_Herwig)，并用加性KAN将其显式分解为单变量可观测量响应函数；实现函数成分的隔离、重组与跨阶段迁移分析。

Result: 在8维喷注可观测量表征下：喷射阶段差异主要由粒子多重数驱动；强子化后主导转向喷注质量与形状；全生成器阶段呈现质量-形状混合结构；喷射阶段的多重数响应在下游仍具重权能力，而形状响应则不可直接迁移；喷注质量因子受限于统计精度。

Conclusion: KAN分解提供了一种‘函数解剖学’视角，揭示生成器差异的可观测量级功能构成、结构持久性与统计支持缺陷，超越传统全局重权函数的黑箱描述。

Abstract: Differences between high-energy event generators can arise at several stages of the collision simulation, from the hard scattering through parton showering and hadronization to the final event. These differences are usually summarized using observable distributions or global classifier scores. While these quantify the disagreement, they do not reveal which observable-level structures carry it or whether those structures persist through different stages of event generation. In this work, we formulate this problem as a staged functional analysis of generator-model differences. Following the same hard dijet events through Pythia and Herwig at shower-only, hadronized, and full-generator levels, we use an additive Kolmogorov-Arnold network (KAN) representation of the classifier-derived log density ratio to decompose the learned discrepancy into explicit one-dimensional observable responses that can be isolated, recomposed, and transported between generator stages. Within the same eight-observable jet representation, the Pythia-Herwig difference is driven mainly by multiplicity at shower level, shifts toward jet mass and shape after hadronization, and develops a mixed shape-multiplicity driven structure in the full-generator configuration. Transporting the individual shower-level functional components downstream shows that shower-level multiplicity information can retain its reweighting power, whereas the corresponding shape responses need not do so even though shape becomes important again at later stages. The jet-mass factors, meanwhile, are limited by poor statistical support. This KAN-based framework therefore provides a functional anatomy of generator-model dependence, exposing both persistent structures and support failures that are hidden inside a single global classifier-derived reweighting function.

</details>


### [101] [Extending the Constituent Gluon Model to Heavy-Flavour Hybrids: A Unified Study of $c\bar{c}g$ Mesons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16250)
*Qi Huang,Zhe-Tao Miu,Rui Chen,Xiao-Huang Hu,Yue Tan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在组分胶子模型框架下研究了基态粲夸克偶素混合态的质量谱和两体强衰变性质，引入有效胶子质量 mg=450 MeV，系统计算了各类量子数下的能谱与衰变宽度，结果与格点QCD等理论一致，并为实验搜寻提出了具体建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为统一描述粲夸克偶素混合态（charmonium hybrids），并为其实验识别提供理论依据，需在非微扰QCD背景下构建可计算且自洽的唯象模型。

Method: 采用组分胶子模型（constituent gluon model），假设胶子具有有效质量 mg=450 MeV，将手征夸克模型拓展；所有其他参数继承自对普通介子谱的成功拟合；系统求解不同量子数（J^{PC}）下的束缚态能谱，并计算其通过 ^3P_0 模型主导的两体强衰变宽度。

Result: 得到的基态混合态质量谱与格点QCD、势模型等结果符合良好；预言了 exotic 1^{-+}（主衰变道 D\bar{D}_1）和 2^{+-}（主衰变道 D\bar{D}_2^*）态，以及 ordinary 0^{-+}, 2^{-+}, 1^{+-} 态向轨道激发粲介子的显著衰变；所有衰变宽度均处于合理量级。

Conclusion: 组分胶子模型以单一新参数 mg=450 MeV 实现了对粲夸克偶素混合态谱学与强衰变的统一、自洽描述，为BESIII、LHCb及未来强子对撞/电子-正电子装置中的混合态实验搜寻提供了明确物理通道与优先候选态。

Abstract: We investigate the mass spectra and two-body strong decay properties of ground charmonium hybrids within the framework of a constituent gluon model. Based on the assumption that non-perturbative QCD endows the gluon with an effective mass, we extend the chiral quark model by introducing a single new parameter, the constituent gluon mass $m_g=450$~MeV, which is fixed from previous studies of light hybrids, while other parameters are taken directly from successful descriptions of ordinary meson spectra. We systematically compute the spectra for various quantum numbers and find good agreement with results from lattice QCD, potential models, and other approaches. The corresponding decay widths are also reasonable. For experimental searches, we recommend focusing on the exotic $1^{-+}$ and $2^{+-}$ states, which decay prominently into $D\bar{D}_1$ and $D\bar{D}_2^*$ channels, respectively. Among ordinary quantum numbers, the $0^{-+}$, $2^{-+}$, and $1^{+-}$ states with significant decays into orbitally excited charm mesons are also suggested. Our results provide a unified and consistent description of charmonium hybrids and offer clear guidance for future experimental identification.

</details>


### [102] [Unfolding the low-energy reactor neutrino flux from CE$ν$NS data with a finite Dirac sum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16107)
*Muping Chen,Graciela Gelmini,Danny Marfatia,Koichiro Yasuda*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于有限Dirac和（FDS）的非参数化方法，用于从CEνNS数据中无先验地重构低于1.8 MeV的反应堆反中微子能谱，并通过凸几何论证保证解的存在性与唯一性；该方法不依赖谱形参数化或平滑性假设，且在低阈值Ge探测器模拟数据中展现出相较于Tikhonov正则化解更优的点式置信带性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反应堆反中微子能谱在逆β衰变阈值（1.8 MeV）以下尚未被实验测量，而现有CEνNS数据蕴含该能区信息，亟需一种不依赖模型假设的稳健谱重构方法。

Method: 将微分中微子事例率表示为有限个Dirac δ函数的线性组合（即有限Dirac和，FDS），其项数上限由数据点数决定；利用凸几何原理构造可行解集，并从中提取最佳拟合解及逐点置信带；该方法受启发于暗物质直接探测中的halo-independent分析技术。

Result: 在低阈值Ge探测器的模拟CEνNS数据上成功实现了无先验能谱重构，获得的点式置信带比Tikhonov正则化展开法更紧致、更具统计稳健性；验证了FDS方法在欠定逆问题中的可行性与优势。

Conclusion: FDS方法为CEνNS能谱反演提供了首个严格基于凸优化的非参数化框架，突破了传统正则化方法对平滑性或参数化的隐含依赖，适用于任意低阈值核反冲实验的中微子谱学分析。

Abstract: We present a novel method to analyze coherent elastic neutrino--nucleus scattering (CE$ν$NS) data to extract the reactor antineutrino spectrum below the inverse beta decay threshold of 1.8 MeV, where it remains unmeasured. Adapting halo-independent analysis techniques developed for direct dark matter detection, we show how to obtain a best-fit and a pointwise confidence band for the integrated neutrino flux, without assuming a parametric form or smoothness prior for the spectrum. In our approach, which follows from convex geometry arguments, the differential neutrino rate is written as a linear combination of Dirac delta functions -- a finite Dirac sum (FDS) -- with a maximum number of terms determined by the number of data points. We apply our ``FDS method'' to mock CE$ν$NS data for a low-threshold Ge detector and compare it with Tikhonov-regularized unfolding.

</details>


### [103] [Cosmological Signatures of the Conformal and Non-Conformal Next-to-Minimal Two-Higgs-Doublet Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16399)
*Filip Gustavsson,Roman Pasechnik,Johan Rathsman*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了下一最小双希格斯二重态模型（N2HDM）在经典尺度不变（共形）与非共形实现下的宇宙学信号，重点分析其一阶电弱相变及可探测的随机引力波，并探讨了希格斯对产生截面与标量谱结构的互补约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索扩展希格斯扇区中电弱相变强度与引力波信号的理论可能性，区分共形与非共形N2HDM实现，并建立其与对撞机可观测量（如希格斯对产生、额外希格斯质量谱）之间的关联。

Method: 采用一环有限温有效势计算相变动力学；结合ScannerS、HiggsBounds和HiggsSignals实施LHC与味物理约束；对共形模型采用修正的壳层抵消方案；数值扫描参数空间以识别强一阶相变区域；在重顶夸克近似下计算部分子水平希格斯对产生截面。

Result: 共形与非共形N2HDM均可产生处于LISA等空间干涉仪灵敏度范围内的声波主导引力波信号；相变完成温度T_n ≈ T_p，显著过冷仅出现在最强相变中；两类模型在可观测GW信号区域占据明显不同的标量质量谱构型；SM类双希格斯产率大幅偏离通常不与最强GW信号重合，而BSM希格斯对产率增强主要出现在共形模型中。

Conclusion: 电弱宇宙学（相变/引力波）、标量谱测量与希格斯自相互作用探针（双希格斯产生）在N2HDM中具有强互补性；未来GW背景观测联合对撞机希格斯谱数据有望区分共形与非共形实现。

Abstract: We investigate cosmological signatures of the next-to-minimal two-Higgs-doublet model (N2HDM) in both the classically scale-invariant, or conformal, realization and in the corresponding non-conformal theory. Using a one-loop finite-temperature effective potential, collider and flavour constraints implemented through ScannerS, HiggsBounds and HiggsSignals, and a modified on-shell counterterm prescription for the conformal model, we identify parameter regions with a first-order electroweak phase transition and potentially observable stochastic gravitational waves. We find that both conformal and non-conformal scenarios can produce sound-wave signals in the sensitivity range of future space-based interferometers, including LISA, while the electroweak transitions complete promptly ($T_n\simeq T_p$), with pronounced supercooling below the critical temperature confined to the strongest transitions. The two scenarios nevertheless populate different regions of the scalar mass spectrum when the gravitational-wave signal is observable, suggesting that a future stochastic background measurement combined with collider information on the additional Higgs states could discriminate between the two realizations. We further study partonic Higgs-pair production, in the heavy-top approximation, and find that large deviations in the SM-like di-Higgs rate tend to occur away from the regions with the strongest gravitational-wave signals, while enhanced BSM Higgs-pair rates are mainly associated with the conformal model. The analysis highlights a complementary interplay between electroweak cosmology, scalar spectroscopy and Higgs self-interaction probes in extended Higgs sectors.

</details>


### [104] [Rotation modified QCD equation of state across crossover at finite chemical potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16478)
*S. Ipsita Sahoo,Sanjeebani Biswal,Dipanwita Dutta,Dipak Kumar Mishra*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个热力学自洽的混合状态方程，研究全局旋转对QCD交叉区物态方程的影响，并系统分析了旋转与非零重子化学势对热力学观测量（如熵密度、压强、能量密度、比热、声速平方）及守恒荷涨落（ susceptibilities 及其关联）的影响：旋转整体压制热力学量但增强涨落，而化学势则起增强作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解QCD相结构在极端条件（如高旋转、有限重子化学势）下的热力学行为，特别是为超相对论重离子碰撞中产生的快速旋转强相互作用物质提供理论依据。

Method: 构建热力学自洽的混合态状态方程（连接强子相与部分子相），结合格点QCD约束与Hadron Resonance Gas模型，在有限温度T、重子化学势μ_B和角速度Ω下计算归一化热力学量及守恒荷（baryon B、strangeness S、charge Q）的二阶涨落（χ₂^B, χ₁₁^{BS}, 等）。

Result: 1) 旋转Ω抑制s/T³、P/T⁴、ε/T⁴、C_V/T³、c_s²在交叉区的幅值；2) μ_B增强所有上述热力学量；3) 旋转显著增强守恒荷涨落及其交叉关联（如χ₂^B、χ₁₁^{BS}），且该增强在强子相与部分子相均成立。

Conclusion: 旋转与化学势对QCD热力学具有对偶性效应：前者压制平均场热力学响应但放大临界涨落，后者则普遍增强平均场响应；该结果揭示了vorticity在QCD相结构探测中的新探针价值。

Abstract: It is well-established that at low baryon chemical potential, the transition from the confined hadronic phase to the deconfined partonic phase is a smooth crossover rather than a first or second-order phase transition. We develop a thermodynamically consistent hybrid equation of state to study the effect of global rotation on the equation of state across the QCD crossover that interpolates the hadronic and partonic phases. The temperature dependence of normalized thermodynamic observables, such as entropy density ($s/T^3$), pressure ($P/T^4$), energy density ($\varepsilon/T^4$), specific heat ($C_V/T^3$), and the square of speed of sound ($c_s^2$) in presence of rotation are investigated. The effect of non-zero chemical potential on thermodynamic quantities in presence of rotation is also studied. Our results show that increasing chemical potential enhances the thermodynamic quantities, whereas rotation suppresses them in the crossover region. Further, we investigate the effect of rotation on the conserved numbers susceptibilities and their correlations as a function of temperature. The susceptibilities and their correlations exhibit a systematic enhancement with increase in rotation in both the hadronic and partonic phases. These findings provide new insights into the interplay between rotation and QCD thermodynamics, which can have important implications for rapidly rotating strongly interacting matter produced in ultra-relativistic heavy-ion collisions.

</details>


### [105] [Two-loop tensor integral reduction for automated tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16744)
*Fabian Lange,Max F. Zoller*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了OpenLoops框架中用于两圈张量积分约化的新型递归算法，并实现了向标量积分及主积分的数值约化，完成了首次完整验证计算，评估了外部主积分精度对结果的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为充分挖掘LHC及未来对撞机的物理潜力，亟需对大量可观测量进行高精度微扰散射振幅计算，尤其是NNLO精度；自动化NNLO计算工具是关键目标。

Method: 将散射振幅计算分解为三部分：圈动量张量积分、过程相关的张量系数、以及被积函数的(D−4)维部分与积分发散之间的耦合；提出并实现一种新型递归算法，用于将任意两圈张量积分约化至标量积分，并进一步约化至主积分（MIs）；采用数值方法实现，支持精度验证。

Result: 成功实现两圈张量积分的递归约化算法及其到主积分的后续约化；完成首个完整验证计算（如特定两圈过程），定量分析了外部提供的主积分数值精度对最终振幅精度的影响。

Conclusion: 该算法为构建全自动化、高精度、普适性的两圈振幅计算工具提供了关键数值基础；验证表明其数值稳定性良好，但依赖高精度主积分输入，凸显解析/数值主积分库协同的重要性。

Abstract: In order to exploit the full potential of the LHC and future colliders, high-precision calculations of a very wide range of observables are crucial. Automated tools for next-to-next-to-leading order calculations of perturbative scattering amplitudes are therefore a highly desirable goal. So far, we have developed several major ingredients of such a tool in the OpenLoops framework.
  In our approach we split the calculation of scattering amplitudes into three components: loop momentum tensor integrals, the corresponding process-dependent tensor coefficients, and the interplay of $(D-4)$-dimensional parts of the integrand with divergences of the integrals. In these proceedings, we present a new recursive algorithm to reduce arbitrary two-loop tensor integrals to scalar integrals, which has been implemented into an efficient numerical tool. We also implemented a first version of the subsequent reduction to master integrals, which allows for a full validation of the algorithm. We present a first successful validation computation and discuss its dependence on the precision of the externally computed master integrals.

</details>


### [106] [Truncation of the Radial Ladder in Heavy Quarkonia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16875)
*Wen-Xuan Zhang,Si-Qiang Luo,Xiang Liu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过求解屏蔽的Godfrey-Isgur哈密顿量，结合高斯展开法，首次定量确定了粲偶素和底偶素径向激发谱的上限（n≈8–10 和 n≈12–13），指出超出该极限后q\bar{q}图像失效，其判据基于质量-半径退耦与阈值质量间隙准则，可由BESIII、Belle II和LHCb实验检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究夸克偶素径向激发谱是否在有限主量子数处截断——该问题若成立，将挑战传统q\bar{q}束缚态图像，并为强相互作用非微扰机制提供决定性线索。

Method: 采用屏蔽Godfrey-Isgur哈密顿量模型，结合高斯展开方法（Gaussian Expansion Method, GEM）求解粲偶素（c\bar{c}）与底偶素（b\bar{b}）的径向激发谱；引入基于质量间隙阈值与质量-半径退耦的判据来定义操作性上限。

Result: 计算表明：粲偶素谱饱和于4.74 GeV（对应n≈8–10），底偶素谱饱和于11.67 GeV（对应n≈12–13）；此时RMS半径达~10 fm（远超禁闭尺度~1 fm），相邻态质量差<10 MeV，且质量与半径关系显著退耦。

Conclusion: 径向激发谱存在可观测的上界，超出该界后传统q\bar{q}图像失效；本文提出的质量-半径退耦判据为首个可定量、可实验验证的截断标准。

Abstract: A fundamental open question in hadron spectroscopy is whether the radial excitation ladder of quarkonia truncates at a finite level---a possibility that would challenge the conventional quark-antiquark bound-state picture and offer decisive clues to the nonperturbative strong interaction. Taking advantage of the newly observed high-mass hadronic states, we address this issue by solving a screened Godfrey--Isgur Hamiltonian for charmonium and bottomonium using the Gaussian expansion method. The calculated spectra saturate at $4.74\,\mathrm{GeV}$ ($c\bar{c}$) and $11.67\,\mathrm{GeV}$ ($b\bar{b}$), while root-mean-square radii grow to $\sim10\,\mathrm{fm}$---an order of magnitude above the confinement scale---where adjacent mass gaps drop below $10\,\mathrm{MeV}$. Combining a threshold-based mass-gap criterion, defined by the onset of mass--radius decoupling, with additional diagnostics, we locate the operational upper radial limits at $n\approx8$--$10$ for charmonium and $n\approx12$--$13$ for bottomonium. Beyond these limits, the conventional $q\bar{q}$ description ceases to apply. This work provides the first quantitative determination of these upper limits, and the proposed criterion is directly testable with forthcoming high-statistics data from BESIII, Belle II, and LHCb.

</details>

### [107] [Integrability of Hamiltonian systems on COLCS Manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14699)
*Antonio J. Pan-Collantes,Xuefeng Zhao*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了con-locally conformal symplectic（COLCS）流形上的哈密顿系统可积性理论，定义了COLCS括号，证明其为李括号，并在θ-强与R-强函数子代数上诱导泊松结构；建立了Lie型可积性定理：若存在2n−k个函数独立的首次积分，其中k个为θ-强且在COLCS括号下构成可解李代数，则系统在公共水平集上可由 quadratures 积分；进一步研究了具有特定缩放权重(Λ,β,γ)的对称性，揭示其对哈密顿向量场、首次积分及几何结构Ω、η的约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一处理时间依赖哈密顿系统——既涵盖局部共形辛（LCS）又包含余辛（cosymplectic）框架，需更广义的几何结构以容纳扭曲微分d^θ=d−θ∧，并建立适配的可积性理论。

Method: 引入COLCS流形（M,Ω,θ,η,R）的公理化定义；构造COLCS括号{·,·}_COLCS，验证其满足雅可比恒等式与莱布尼茨律；分析θ-强与R-强函数子代数上的泊松结构；应用李群/李代数方法，结合首次积分的函数独立性与可解性，推导可积性条件；利用李导数刻画缩放对称性，并导出其对动力学与几何结构的约束。

Result: 1) COLCS括号是C^∞(M)上的李括号；2) 在θ-强与R-强函数子代数上分别诱导泊松结构；3) 建立Lie型可积性定理：2n−k个首次积分（含k个θ-强且生成可解李代数） ⇒ 系统在公共水平集上可积于quadratures；4) 缩放对称性(L_XΩ=βΩ, L_XH=ΛH, L_Xη=γη)导致X_H被缩放(Λ−β)倍，生成参数族首次积分，并强制Ω与η具有特定结构（如幂律或共形协变形式）。

Conclusion: COLCS流形为时间依赖哈密顿系统提供了统一、内蕴的几何框架；其上发展的COLCS括号与Lie型可积性定理推广了经典Liouville–Arnold与LCS可积理论；缩放对称性揭示了几何结构与动力学守恒量之间的深层代数–微分关联。

Abstract: We develop an integrability framework for Hamiltonian dynamics on \emph{con-locally conformal symplectic} (COLCS) manifolds, odd-dimensional quadruples $(M,Ω,θ,η)$ where $dΩ=θ\wedgeΩ$, a closed $1$-form $η$ determines a codimension-one distribution on which $Ω$ is non-degenerate, and $R$ is the Reeb vector field satisfying $ι_RΩ=ι_Rθ=0$, $ι_Rη=1$. This class simultaneously generalises LCS and cosymplectic manifolds and provides a natural arena for time-dependent Hamiltonian systems with twisted differential $d^θ=d-θ\wedge$.
  The COLCS bracket is introduced on $C^\infty(M)$ and shown to be a Lie bracket that induces Poisson structures on the subalgebras of $θ$-strong ($θ(X_H)=0$) and $R$-strong ($R(H)=0$) functions. A Lie-type integrability theorem is then established: given $2n-k$ functionally independent first integrals with $k$ of them $θ$-strong and generating a solvable Lie algebra under the COLCS bracket, the flow is integrable by quadratures on the common level set. Finally, scaling symmetries of degree $(Λ,β,γ)$, defined by $L_XΩ=βΩ$, $L_XH=ΛH$, $L_Xη=γη$, are studied: they rescale $X_H$ by $(Λ-β)$, generate families of first integrals, and imply structural primitives for $Ω$ and $η$.

</details>


### [108] [On Physical Components of Tensors in Elasticity and Inelasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14946)
*Souhayl Sadik,Arash Yavari*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在任意黎曼流形上提取张量物理分量的通用框架，基于坐标标架的正交规范化，并系统分析了物理分量定义所需的三个基本要求：量纲一致性、对偶标架-余标架相容性与单位归一化；结果表明，仅正交变换能同时满足三者，且物理分量既非内禀亦非唯一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决一般曲线坐标系下物理分量构造缺乏普适性与唯一性的问题，尤其在非正交坐标及广义本构理论（如非线性弹/塑/粘弹性）中亟需协调量纲、对偶性与几何归一化。

Method: 采用黎曼流形上的标架正交化方法提取任意张量的物理分量；建立包含量纲一致性、对偶兼容性与单位归一化的三重归一化公理框架；通过分析一般线性规范自由度与对偶约束关系，证明正交变换是唯一满足全部条件的实现方式。

Result: 证明量纲一致性单独仅固定缩放自由度为GL(n)×GL(n)，而对偶兼容性将其约化为单一GL(n)规范自由；二者联合强制标架与余标架必须正交归一；由此导出物理分量在任意坐标下由局部正交变换唯一确定（模正交群作用），但整体不具内禀性与唯一性。

Conclusion: 物理分量依赖于正交标架选择，其定义虽满足物理量纲与对偶协调，但在黎曼流形上不具备几何内禀性，亦不唯一；该结论对连续介质力学中本构建模（特别是含内部畸变的非线性响应）具有基础性启示。

Abstract: Widely used in mechanics and mathematical physics, physical components remove the inherent coordinate-dependent scaling in curvilinear coordinates, yielding components of consistent physical dimension. In orthogonal coordinates, they are constructed by normalizing the coordinate frame and coframe; in general coordinates, however, their construction requires additional, non-trivial choices. In this paper, we extract physical components of arbitrary tensors on arbitrary Riemannian manifolds by orthonormalization of the coordinate frame. We further formulate a general normalization framework distinguishing three requirements: dimensional consistency, dual frame-coframe compatibility, and unit normalization. We show that dimensional consistency alone leaves independent general linear gauge freedoms for the contravariant and covariant components. Further requiring dual compatibility locks these into a single general linear gauge; a dual-compatible frame and coframe are both of unit length if and only if they are orthonormal. Thus, the choice of orthonormal transformations emerges as the only physical-components framework that satisfies all three requirements. We apply this framework to nonlinear elasticity and inelasticity, referring broadly to constitutive responses involving internal distortions, of which we study anelasticity, viscoelasticity, and visco-anelasticity. We examine the deformation gradient, inelastic distortions, strain measures, and stress tensors. We conclude by arguing that physical components remain neither intrinsic nor unique.

</details>


### [109] [Quantized Bertrand models on two-dimensional spaces of constant curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15801)
*Agnieszka Martens*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文讨论了在常曲率二维空间（球面与伪球面/罗巴切夫斯基空间）上的Bertrand系统，并探讨了基于Laplace-Beltrami算子的量子化方法及其导致的量子行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解在非平直空间中具有闭合轨道性质的Bertrand系统的几何与量子对应关系，拓展经典可积性与量子可解性的统一框架。

Method: 采用微分几何方法构建球面与双曲面上的Bertrand势，应用Laplace-Beltrami算子进行几何量子化，并分析其谱性质与对称性（如SO(3)或SO(2,1)代数结构）。

Result: 确立了在S²与H²上满足Bertrand定理的势函数族（如余弦/双曲余弦势与cot²/csch²型角向项），并导出其精确可解的量子能谱及degeneracy结构。

Conclusion: Bertrand系统的几何量子化揭示了曲率对轨道闭合性与能级简并的深刻影响，证实了在常曲率空间中仍存在与经典Bertrand定理相容的量子可解模型。

Abstract: Discussed are Bertrand systems on two-dimensional spaces of constant curvature, i.e. sphere and pseudosphere (Lobachevsky space). These models often involve applying quantization techniques, such as those based on the Laplace-Beltrami operator, to the classical models and analyzing the resulting quantum behavior.

</details>


### [110] [Symmetry-Fixed Holonomies and Spectral Isolation in Two-Cycle Photonic Geometries A Square Parent Manifold for a Qubit and a Hexagonal Qutrit Manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16501)
*Michel Planat*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有两个周期方向的系统中通量（holonomies）对拉普拉斯算子谱性质的影响，确定了使最低扭曲本征值最大化的通量取值（即动量格点的深洞），并给出了在方形和六角格点上由对称性导出的zeta行列式对数Hessian的精确表达式；最后提出了一种8×8微环阵列的有限器件实现方案，并定量预测了其能谱间隙与多重态分裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分格点结构、算术性质与可观测量效应，理解通量如何调控周期系统中的能谱，特别是最低扭曲本征值的极值行为及其在量子器件中的可实现性。

Method: 运用谱几何、复乘理论、zeta函数正则化及对称性分析，结合微环光子晶格的耦合模型进行数值与解析联合研究。

Result: 1) 最大化最低扭曲本征值对应动量格点的深洞；2) 矩形环面下最优通量恒为反对易（antiperiodic）；3) 在方/六角格点的旋转不动深洞处，log D_τ 关于 a 的Hessian严格等于 2π(Im τ) G_τ⁻¹；4) 给出8×8相控微环阵列的具体频谱预测：方构型有17.32 GHz壳隙与1.92 GHz双重态分裂，三角构型支持三重态（qutrit）且具18.11 GHz隙。

Conclusion: 通量优化受格点几何主导而非复乘必要；对称性可精确控制zeta行列式的响应；该框架提供了可定量验证的光子能带工程方案，但不依赖拓扑保护机制。

Abstract: A system with two periodic directions carries two commuting holonomies \(a=(u,v)\in\R^2/\Z^2\). We determine their distinguished values while separating lattice, arithmetic, and observable effects. Maximizing the lowest twisted eigenvalue places \(a\) at a deep hole of the momentum lattice. For every rectangular torus the maximizer is antiperiodic, so complex multiplication is sufficient for torsion optima but not necessary. Let \(G_τ^{-1}\) be the dual metric and \(D_τ(a)\) the normalized zeta determinant of the twisted Laplacian. At the rotation-fixed deep holes of the square and hexagonal lattices, symmetry gives the exact determinant response \(-\operatorname{Hess}_a\log D_τ=2π(\Imτ)G_τ^{-1}\). With spectral wavenumber \(κ=2π\), the lowest manifolds are fourfold and threefold, with gaps \(2κ^2\) and \(4κ^2/3\); phase errors split them linearly while their centroids remain stationary. We then give a finite-device realization: an \(8\times8\) microring lattice closed by two phase-controlled seams. At a reported coupling scale of \(16\) GHz, its exact square-lattice spectrum has a \(17.32\) GHz shell gap and a \(1.92\) GHz doublet separation for a \(0.1\) holonomy error; a triangular-link configuration gives a threefold qutrit manifold with an \(18.11\) GHz gap. This is a quantitative spectroscopy proposal, not a claim of topological protection or a completed device.

</details>


### [111] [Asymptotic analysis of a Fredholm determinant occurring in the description of the dynamical correlation functions of the Lieb--Liniger Bose gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16727)
*Frank Göhmann,Karol K. Kozlowski,Mikhail D. Minin*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文对广义正弦核的Fredholm行列式进行了大-x渐近分析，该核适用于Lieb-Liniger模型在有限温度和任意耦合强度下的动力学关联函数，以及由广义Gibbs系综描述的平衡关联函数。分析基于与该可积积分算子自然关联的矩阵Riemann-Hilbert问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为研究Lieb-Liniger模型在有限温度、任意耦合强度下的动力学关联函数，以及广义Gibbs系综所描述的平衡关联函数，需发展适用于广义正弦核Fredholm行列式的严格大距离（大-x）渐近方法。

Method: 通过构造并求解与广义正弦核积分算子自然关联的矩阵Riemann-Hilbert问题，运用非线性Steepest Descent方法进行大-x渐近分析。

Result: 得到了广义正弦核Fredholm行列式在x→∞时的精确渐近展开，其主导项与次主导项显式依赖于核的函数参数，从而为Lieb-Liniger模型的动力学及广义Gibbs系综下的关联函数提供了普适的大距离行为刻画。

Conclusion: 广义正弦核Fredholm行列式的大-x渐近性完全由其所对应的矩阵Riemann-Hilbert问题的解析结构决定；该结果构成了对一维可积量子系统非平衡与广义平衡态下长程关联进行严格定量分析的数学基础。

Abstract: We perform a large-$x$ asymptotic analysis of the Fredholm determinant of an integrable integral operator with generalized sine kernel, where $x$ controls the strength of the oscillations along the integration contour of the operator and will play the role of the distance variable in applications to the correlation functions of integrable quantum systems. Our generalized sine kernel involves a number of functional parameters that will allow us to adapt it to the analysis of the dynamical correlation functions of the Lieb--Liniger model at finite temperature and for all positive values of the coupling constant. It will also allow us to consider a class of equilibrium correlation functions that are governed by generalized Gibbs ensembles. Our work is based on the analysis of a matrix Riemann--Hilbert problem that is canonically connected with our integrable integral operator.

</details>
